14:I[8669,["/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/08cykig6vtx_p.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/07qi2okp-.yp9.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0frujd.eyhz4o.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0n4z.7yu76je-.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/09-o.77-p0p36.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/1304s0wcp2kce.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0j1uy9t~zxcf0.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/14_5o-nbg8k_r.js"],"DateSidebar"]
15:I[44692,["/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/08cykig6vtx_p.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/07qi2okp-.yp9.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0frujd.eyhz4o.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0n4z.7yu76je-.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/09-o.77-p0p36.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/1304s0wcp2kce.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0j1uy9t~zxcf0.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/14_5o-nbg8k_r.js"],"DailyView"]
16:T472,LazyTrain adds an optimization layer on top of a MegaTrain-like layer-streaming executor. The executor provides primitives (stream parameters CPU→GPU per layer, compute a layer or recompute block, offload gradients GPU→CPU, reload activation checkpoints from GPU/CPU/NVMe). LazyTrain decides, before training starts, which primitives to use and when.

Core idea: formulate activation checkpoint selection, placement (HBM/DRAM/NVMe), recomputation blocks, and communication overlap as a mixed-integer linear program (MILP).
- Binary variables select a legal checkpoint path over layer boundaries via edges (s,e) representing recomputation blocks.
- Binary placement variables assign each materialized boundary to GPU/CPU/NVMe.
- Continuous variables allocate activation traffic into compute windows to model overlap vs exposure.
- Objective minimizes recomputation cost plus incremental exposed communication induced by placement, rather than maximizing offload volume.

They also include a “Hybrid 8-bit operator” that couples 8-bit optimizer states (memory saving) with fast gradient clipping to mitigate added CPU-side overhead.17:T425,Three layers. First, ablations of the scaling-law recipe (parameter counting, embedding inclusion, LR decay, warmup length) show these details matter little compared to whether hyperparameters are fully tuned. Second, the noisy quadratic model (Lourie et al. 2025a) characterizes the hyperparameter loss surface as quadratic, low-rank, and approximately normal near the optimum; as parameters and data grow together, good configurations fill a larger fraction of the space because the intrinsic dimension of the surface drops—the blessing of dimensionality from parameters cures the curse of dimensionality in hyperparameters, with the effective number of hyperparameters approaching one. Third, three tools are combined into a methodology: the noisy quadratic limit diagnoses whether tuning is complete, scaling laws describe loss vs. scale, and the perplexity–capability correspondence checks whether pretraining-loss changes translate into downstream capabilities. The normalization-placement debate (Pre-LN vs Post-LN) is used as a worked demonstration.18:T60e,CLR is a training-free two-stage framework. In Stage 1, the model samples K full solution traces. Each trace must output not only a final answer but also exactly M decision-critical claims: concise intermediate propositions whose failure would undermine the answer. These are meant to be semantic anchors such as key constraints, transformations, maximality conditions, or intermediate conclusions, not generic summaries or answer restatements. The purpose is to compress a long reasoning trace into a small set of high-information, checkable propositions. In Stage 2, the same model is not asked to solve the problem again. Instead, it receives only the original problem and the extracted claims, and it actively searches for contradictions, counterexamples, missing conditions, factual mistakes, or unsupported inferences. The central design choice is falsification rather than proof. There is a useful asymmetry here: constructing a fully correct solution is hard, but invalidating a wrong trace only requires finding one decisive flaw. After verification, CLR maps claim-level verdicts into a nonlinear trace-level reliability score and aggregates answer groups by total reliability. This allows a reliable minority to overturn an incorrect majority. Relative to self-consistency, the method reallocates part of the budget from generating more traces to verifying the semantic anchors of existing traces. Relative to step-level verification, it avoids exhaustive checking and does not require process supervision or a separately trained verifier.19:T4ca,The evaluation spans four LLMs and multiple difficult reasoning benchmarks, with comparisons made under matched model-call budgets. The budget accounting is clean: CLR@K uses K generation calls plus K verification calls, matching the 2K generation calls of Cons@2K. Two headline results are especially informative. On HMMT25, Gemma-4-12B-it improves from 76.67% with Cons@64 to 88.75% with CLR@32, a gain of 12.08 points under the same call budget. On CMIMC25, GPT-OSS-20B improves from 77.50% with Cons@64 to 82.19% with CLR@32 while using 37.0% fewer generated tokens, and it outperforms Pass@1 by 27.15 points. The paper’s stronger point is not just accuracy but token efficiency: Stage 2 verifies compressed claims instead of regenerating long chains, so the method can improve both correctness and compute efficiency. The qualitative analysis argues that whole-trace scoring is easily dominated by long stretches of routine reasoning, whereas claim-level falsification exposes decisive errors more directly. The most relevant ablations are likely around the fixed number of claims, whether the original trace should be hidden during verification, and why nonlinear reliability mapping works better than simple counting.1a:T519,The work adapts Generalizability Theory (G-theory) from psychometrics to the long-horizon agent evaluation setting, building a fully crossed four-facet variance decomposition model with facets for agent, task, trajectory step, and error category. It decomposes total variance in binary success observations into the agent main effect (the true general capability signal), independent main effects for each facet, pairwise interaction effects between facets, and residual variance. Three independent variance estimators are used to cross-validate results: Henderson Method-I, restricted maximum likelihood implemented via lme4, and a Bayesian binomial generalized linear mixed model run on GPU. Building on the G-study (variance component estimation), a D-study (decision study) translates variance components into a budget frontier of minimum tasks, steps, and judges required to meet target reliability thresholds (generalizability coefficient Eρ² ≥ 0.85 for relative ranking decisions, dependability coefficient Φ ≥ 0.90 for absolute threshold decisions such as SLA gates). The final DDR reporting specification combines a variance component table, evaluation size budget frontier, cost-adjusted reliability index, difficulty-conditional reliability profile, and capability ceiling decomposition.1b:T610,Full variance decomposition is run across three public enterprise agent benchmarks (TheAgentCompany, τ²-bench, AppWorld) and one auxiliary failure-mode dataset (MAST/MAD), with five core empirical findings: 1. Across all datasets and check types, the agent main effect accounts for less than 3% of total variance, while the agent-by-task interaction accounts for 7-23% of variance (4-13x larger than the main effect), meaning rank differences are driven primarily by fit to specific tasks rather than cross-task general capability. 2. Reliability collapses sharply on harder tasks: for action checks on τ²-bench, aggregate Eρ² is 0.752, but Eρ² drops to 0.000 on the hardest quartile of tasks, so aggregate reliability provides no valid guarantee for hard-task deployments. 3. Across 50 random splits, Eρ² estimated on training splits correlates negatively with held-out Eρ² (r=-0.90), meaning reliability projections from observed samples systematically overestimate the reliability of replication runs. 4. While the aggregate capability gap ratio is stable at 0.35-0.40 across datasets, per-task-family rankings invert completely (Spearman ρ = -0.50), so population-level capability diagnostics transfer but family-level rank claims do not. 5. Per-category agent-by-task interaction variance is orthogonal to per-category failure frequency (ρ=-0.20), meaning the failure categories where agents differ most are not the categories where failures occur most often. Results from the three variance estimators agree to three decimal places.1c:T581,BenchDrift constructs meaning-preserving benchmark variations and attributes every correctness flip to the specific transformation that caused it. The pipeline has four roles: a generator proposes rephrasings, a validator checks that the original ground-truth answer still holds, the target model answers the original and validated variants, and a judge scores the outputs. This separation is important because it isolates wording effects from genuine changes in problem semantics.

Variations are organized along four axes: linguistic, referential, pragmatic, and structural. The paper defines positive drift as a wrong-to-right flip and negative drift as a right-to-wrong flip. Both are measured over the same denominator, the original problem set size, which makes the two directions directly comparable and allows exact decomposition into reported accuracy, best-case accuracy, and worst-case accuracy. That is a stronger framing than standard robustness reporting because it captures both hidden capability and hidden brittleness.

Methodologically, the paper also includes two important controls: one to test whether rephrasings merely simplify the problems, and another to test whether drift only affects already uncertain examples. In addition, the generator, validator, and judge are swapped across different language models to check that the observed drift is not an artifact of the instrumentation.1d:T52c,The experiments cover 8 models and 3 benchmarks: GSM8K, MMLU, and MATH-Hard. The main empirical result is that drift is substantial in both directions across models and tasks. A single reported benchmark score is therefore only one point inside a wider interval defined by best-case and worst-case performance under meaning-preserving rephrasings.

A second key result is that phrasing sensitivity does not track baseline accuracy in a simple way. Stronger models are not uniformly more stable. Some high-performing models still lose many correct answers under specific transformation types. The paper also studies whether different models fail on similar transformations, which helps distinguish systematic sensitivity from random noise. The evidence suggests that at least part of the drift is structured and transformation-dependent rather than purely stochastic.

The reliability checks with swapped generator, validator, and judge models support the claim that the effect is not a pipeline artifact. The transformation-level attribution tables are especially useful because they show which kinds of rephrasing tend to reveal hidden capability and which tend to expose fragility. The abstract does not provide many headline percentages, so the full paper is best read through its drift-rate tables and breakdown figures.1e:T5fc,The evaluation covers 56,476 inferences: 4 models, 2,017 items, and 7 budgets. Non-monotone behavior appears in roughly 3% to 19% of items under the main controlled analyses, and reaches 25.8% in some all-item settings. It is also model-specific: the same item rarely induces overthinking across models, with cross-model overlap around 6% to 14%. Model rankings reverse across budgets. On GSM8K, LLaMA-3.3 70B leads at 256 tokens with 62.4% accuracy, while GPT-OSS 20B reaches 94.8% at 4,096 tokens and becomes the strongest model under that budget. On GPQA, LLaMA-3 8B is nominally first at 512 tokens with 21.2%, while GPT-OSS 20B reaches 51.0% at 4,096 tokens, close to another model at 50.0%; the small GPQA sample limits significance at that endpoint, but several intermediate reversals are significant. Truncation is a major confound: Qwen-3 32B remains truncated on 59.7% of GPQA items even at 4,096 tokens, while LLaMA-3.3 70B is truncated on only 0.5%. Oracle analysis finds large complementarity. On GPQA, the oracle exceeds the best single model by up to 27.8 percentage points. On GSM8K, the oracle gap peaks at +16.9 points at 256 tokens and then declines as models converge. The budget-aware router improves cross-domain GPQA performance by +2.67 points over the best-per-budget baseline, with a 95% confidence interval of [0.94, 4.40], capturing 14.1% of the oracle gap. Budget features help within-domain by +1.6 to +5.7 points but hurt cross-domain transfer by 1.2 points, indicating domain-specific budget effects.1f:T43c,The Apodex Discovery framework defines the core operational unit of discoverative AI as a heavy-duty solver, which relies on four supporting infrastructure components: 1. Problem formulation, which translates ambiguous open human goals into explicit objectives, constraints, assumptions, and success criteria; 2. Reality-based environments, which provide access to data, tools, compute, experimental interfaces, and human interaction channels required for problem solving; 3. Verification mechanisms that support both outcome and process verification, producing reproducible checks of the correctness and relevance of intermediate claims and final outputs, with support for blind trajectory auditing; 4. Repair loops that feed signals from computation, experiment feedback, external evaluation, and human judgment back to the solver to support failure localization, hypothesis revision, and approach iteration. The accompanying TRACES reality benchmark shifts the unit of analysis from individual answers or single-task success/failure to full end-to-end discovery iteration episodes.20:T891,GAS has two main ingredients.

The first is a decoupled Mixture-of-Transformers architecture. The model uses a shared lower trunk and then splits into an understanding branch and a generation branch. The understanding branch handles standard MLLM tasks such as VQA, captioning, and reasoning through text generation. The generation branch is dedicated to visual generative supervision. The motivation is clear: if both objectives fully share upper-layer parameters, dense generation losses can interfere with the language-centric reasoning path, especially for tasks closer to reconstruction, editing, or dense prediction. By decoupling the higher layers, generation gradients mainly improve the shared lower-level visual representations while reducing direct disruption to the understanding-side decision process. At inference time, the generation branch is removed entirely, so the deployed model has zero extra inference cost.

The second ingredient is the choice of generation target: Next Embedding Prediction rather than discrete visual token prediction or pixel-space diffusion. The generation branch autoregressively predicts continuous visual embeddings, and these embeddings live in the same representation space consumed by the understanding backbone. This is the key design choice. In many unified models, the generation side optimizes a different target space, such as a discrete codebook or a pixel decoder, so the learned signal does not transfer cleanly to understanding. NEP shortens that transfer path by supervising the shared representation space directly. The model is not merely learning to generate images; it is learning to predict the visual representations that the understanding model itself relies on.

On the data side, the paper builds about 10 million generation samples across five major categories and fifteen subtasks, spanning pixel-level perception to higher-level reasoning, using an automated synthesis pipeline. The paper is therefore not only about adding more data. It is also about analyzing which generation tasks help which understanding tasks, where supervision should be injected, and whether parameter isolation really reduces objective conflict.21:T5d7,The experimental findings have three layers. First, GAS improves aggregate visual understanding on both 2B and 4B backbones, which suggests the effect is not tied to a single model size. Second, the gains are task-dependent rather than uniform. Generation tasks with stronger latent correlation to understanding produce larger improvements, and combining multiple task types tends to yield complementary gains across perception-heavy and reasoning-heavy benchmarks. Third, the paper includes a fairly strong analysis suite: parameter-isolation studies, layer-wise supervision injection, per-task contribution analysis, representation diagnostics, matched-budget controls, and repeated runs. That matters because the paper is trying to explain why the method works, not only report a final score.

The hard numbers visible from the provided content are these: training cost is about 11.6% higher in GPU-hours than the baseline, the generation dataset is about 10M samples, and inference overhead is zero because the generation branch is discarded. The excerpt does not include the full benchmark tables, so I would not invent exact gains on individual datasets. When reading the paper, the most important checks are: whether the matched-budget controls are fair, whether decoupled branches outperform fully shared alternatives in a way that supports the gradient-conflict story, and whether NEP is actually better than other generation targets rather than just a convenient implementation choice.22:T4e0,Read this paper with two questions in mind. First, why does generation often fail to help understanding in unified multimodal models? Second, what kind of generation target transfers most directly into the understanding pathway? Start with the introduction, where the authors frame these two tensions clearly. Then go to the architecture figure and the NEP formulation. The real novelty is not just the removable generation branch. It is the decision to place generative supervision in the same continuous representation space used by the understanding model.

In the experiments, prioritize four parts: the main benchmark table, the matched-budget controls, the shared-vs-decoupled ablations, and the per-task contribution analysis. If you care about multimodal pretraining design, the representation-level diagnostics are also worth reading because they help distinguish whether the gains come from better visual fidelity, better cross-modal retention, or both. A 40 to 50 minute skim should be enough to capture the main argument. If you want to assess transfer to audio tokens, video latents, or AR-plus-diffusion style joint modeling, then the appendix on data construction and layer-wise supervision placement is the next place to spend time.23:T4ef,The method is deliberately lightweight and training-free. Instead of training a separate verifier, it reads how much each generated token attends back to visual tokens and uses that as a proxy for visual reference usage. The scoring has two levels. First, token-level calibration: each token is scored using both its generation likelihood and its visual lookback strength. This matters because plain likelihood over-rewards generic fluent tokens, while many visually decisive words are object, attribute, or relation tokens. Second, response-level aggregation: token scores are not simply averaged. They are aggregated under a visual relevance distribution that gives more weight to tokens with stronger visual reference usage. This separation is one of the paper’s better design choices. It distinguishes whether a token is visually supported from how much that token should matter for the final response score. Compared with prior approaches, LookBack avoids extra reward models, preference labels, and additional verification passes. Compared with pure confidence scoring, it explicitly injects image sensitivity. Compared with vision-only heuristics, it still preserves the model’s own uncertainty signal rather than replacing it with raw attention alone.24:T871,方法分两部分：测量协议和缓解方法。

先看测量。作者在训练环中每 20 step 对 OOD evaluation set 做一次双 judge 评估：一个是训练时使用的 proxy judge，另一个是更强、且跨家族的 gold judge。这里“跨家族”很关键，目的是降低与训练 judge 同源偏差带来的假阳性。如果只是 judge 有固定偏差，gold 曲线应整体平移；而作者观察到的是 proxy 持续上升、gold 先升后降，这更符合 reward hacking 而不是静态偏差。实验用了两个独立 train→eval 配对：RubricHub-Medical → HealthBench-Hard，以及 RubricHub-Science → ResearchQA。

缓解方法是 Rubric Dropout。训练时对每个 prompt 的 rubric，随机丢弃一部分 criterion，再用剩余 criterion 计算 reward。这样策略在不同 step 面对的是不断变化的子 rubric，无法稳定利用某个固定 criterion，尤其是那些跨 prompt 重复出现的模板化 criterion，比如“结构清晰”“语言明确”。这个设计的优点是简单：只改 reward function 一行，不增加 judge 调用次数，只有一个超参数 f，即 dropout fraction。

关键实现点在于和 GRPO 的兼容。GRPO 依赖同一 prompt 的多条 rollout 之间 reward 可比较。如果每条 rollout 用不同子 rubric，group-relative advantage 就会被污染。作者因此采用 group-shared masking：同一个 rollout group 共享一张 dropout mask。附录里给出推导，说明在这种设置下 reward normalizer 会在 advantage 中抵消；在 group standardization 之前，dropout 主要表现为对期望 advantage 的缩放，以及对依赖单一 criterion 的更新起到方差正则作用。这个解释很重要，因为它说明 Rubric Dropout 不是在“加噪声碰碰运气”，而是在系统性削弱对单一可利用 criterion 的过拟合。

作者还把 Rubric Dropout 与 POW3R 风格的 criterion reweighting 做了对比。后者试图按训练 usefulness 重加权 criterion，但在这里反而更容易把优化压力集中到少数可利用 criterion 上，因此可能加剧 hacking。全文实验也支持这一点。25:T866,The method has two parts: an in-loop measurement protocol and a mitigation.

For measurement, every 20 training steps the model is evaluated on an OOD benchmark using two judges: the training-time proxy judge and a stronger gold judge from a different family. The cross-family choice is important because it reduces the chance that the observed gap is just shared bias. If the proxy judge were merely biased, the gold curve would be shifted but not necessarily turn downward while the proxy keeps improving. The paper uses two independent train-to-eval pairs: RubricHub-Medical to HealthBench-Hard, and RubricHub-Science to ResearchQA.

The mitigation is Rubric Dropout. At each training step, a random fraction f of the rubric criteria is dropped before computing reward. The policy therefore never sees exactly the same rubric twice, which makes it harder to exploit any fixed criterion, especially generic templates repeated across prompts such as “well-organized” or “clear language.” The appeal is operational simplicity: one line in the reward function, one hyperparameter, and no extra judge calls.

The crucial systems detail is GRPO compatibility. In GRPO, multiple rollouts for the same prompt must be scored on a comparable basis. If each rollout were graded with a different sub-rubric, the group-relative advantage would be corrupted. The paper solves this with group-shared masking: one dropout mask per rollout group. The appendix shows that under this scheme the reward normalizer cancels out of the advantage, and before group standardization the main effect of dropout is to rescale expected advantage while acting as a variance regularizer against updates that rely too heavily on any single criterion. That makes the method more than just noise injection; it is a targeted way to reduce overfitting to exploitable proxy features.

The paper also compares Rubric Dropout with POW3R-style criterion reweighting. The argument is that reweighting can inadvertently concentrate optimization pressure on a smaller set of exploitable criteria, which may worsen hacking rather than reduce it. The experiments support that concern.26:T67b,The most important empirical result is the divergence between proxy and gold curves during training. Using Qwen3-8B with GRPO, the paper observes this on two independent domains. On the medical setup, the proxy-minus-gold gap grows from 29% to 44%. On the science setup evaluated on ResearchQA, the gold score drops by 22 points from its peak while the proxy score continues to rise. That is strong evidence that the standard rubric-RL recipe can over-optimize the proxy out of distribution.

In the fixed three-way comparison, the paper evaluates base training against Rubric Dropout with f = 30% and f = 50%. Both dropout settings outperform base on the gold score at every matched checkpoint within the comparison window. The gain is about +1 to +2 points on HealthBench-Hard and +6 to +7 points on ResearchQA. At the same time, both hacking measures improve, and the paper reports no in-domain cost. That last point matters because anti-hacking interventions often trade away the optimized training metric.

A sweep over dropout fraction shows a broad usable region from 20% to 50%, with the sign turning only at 60%. That suggests the method is not overly fragile to hyperparameter choice. By contrast, POW3R-style reweighting performs below base in the medical sweep and yields the highest overclaim fraction, consistent with the idea that criterion reweighting can amplify proxy exploitation.

The paper also reports consistent window-mean improvements at two model sizes. The presentation emphasizes consistency of direction across domains and scales rather than a single best checkpoint, which is the right way to argue for a training-dynamics claim.27:T702,The method has two layers. The first is the diagnostic metric, Contextual Preference Gain (CPG). For the same response pair, the model is evaluated with and without additional helpful context. CPG measures how much the preference margin increases once that context is added. The margin follows the usual DPO or Bradley-Terry view, based on the relative reward or log-probability gap between preferred and dispreferred responses. If a model truly leverages context, the correct response should become more strongly preferred when grounding information is available, so CPG should be positive and meaningfully large. Empirically, the authors show that higher CPG is strongly associated with lower hallucination, while standard DPO and several variants produce CPG distributions concentrated near zero.

The second layer is C²-DPO. On top of the original preference objective, the authors add a calibration term that explicitly rewards the preference margin under enriched context being larger than the margin without that context. This is the key difference from vanilla DPO. The change is not about a new reward parameterization; it is about writing context sensitivity directly into the optimization target. The same idea is also attached to SimPO and RDPO, which suggests that the proposal is a fairly general calibration add-on rather than a method tied to one specific DPO implementation.

Mechanistically, the method is well matched to settings where the preferred answer is only clearly preferred once grounding evidence is present. If chosen and rejected responses are already easy to separate without extra context, the gain may be limited. But for visual grounding, factual evidence, and other evidence-conditioned preference tasks, the objective is well aligned with the failure mode.28:T5aa,The experimental story is straightforward. First, the paper validates CPG as a diagnostic: models with higher CPG consistently show lower hallucination rates. That gives the proposed optimization direction a credible empirical basis. Second, C²-DPO reduces hallucination on multimodal benchmarks by a clear margin. The headline numbers reported in the paper are a 36% reduction in response-level hallucination rate and a 60% reduction in mention-level hallucination rate on Object HalBench, with consistent gains on HallusionBench as well. Third, the paper checks whether this comes at the cost of general multimodal ability and reports that performance on ScienceQA, MM-Vet, and TextVQA is preserved.

Two additional results are worth noting. The calibration idea transfers to SimPO and RDPO, which supports the claim that the issue is objective-level rather than implementation-specific. The authors also test a text-only setting and find improved factual alignment there too, suggesting that context blindness is a broader weakness of DPO-style training, not only a multimodal problem.

The provided full-content excerpt does not include the complete hyperparameter tables or all model-scale details, so I would not infer exact training sizes beyond the benchmark-level results already stated. When reading the paper, the main tables to verify are the CPG correlation plots, the hallucination benchmarks, and the cross-objective transfer results.29:T469,1) Diagnostic metric: Proposes dimension-corrected Principal-Subspace Overlap. For each pretrained linear weight, SVD is performed to extract top-k left and right singular vectors as principal subspaces. The stepwise realized update (ΔW, including effects of optimizer, learning rate, and momentum) is decomposed into four orthogonal blocks, and the expected overlap under an isotropic null hypothesis is subtracted to obtain excess overlap. 2) Optimization method: Proposes GCPO (Geometrically Constrained Policy Optimization). During each rollout iteration, a hard bilateral orthogonal constraint is applied to the effective update of each adapted layer—forcing updates to lie in the bilateral orthogonal complement of the selected pretrained principal subspaces. This guarantees that the layer's response to inputs in the pretrained principal input subspace remains completely unchanged, while retaining a high-dimensional complement space for task adaptation. The constraint is complementary to KL regularization: KL controls policy divergence in output space, while GCPO controls feasible directions of parameter updates.2a:T432,The study tests the hypothesis in both pretraining and supervised fine-tuning. In pretraining, the token budget, data, model configuration, and optimization setup are fixed, while the training context window is varied. Coherent document span length serves as a proxy for the amount of available relevant information. This design separates longer-context learning from simply seeing more tokens. In supervised fine-tuning, the context length is fixed and the amount of task-relevant context is directly controlled, isolating relevance from length. The theoretical account treats language modeling as compression with two predictive channels: information stored in parameters and information supplied in context. When context offers a lower-complexity solution, less task information needs to be encoded in the weights to reach the same risk threshold. The mechanistic analyses then connect this to lower-complexity contextual solutions, a shift in update pressure from feed-forward networks toward attention modules, and stronger reliance on context tokens at inference time.2b:T712,The proposed audit, J-Access, measures how accessible target knowledge remains along the model’s output pathway. Instead of relying on a standard logit lens, which directly decodes intermediate residual states with the unembedding matrix, the method uses a Jacobian lens. The motivation is specific: after unlearning, the geometry of the residual stream may shift, so the shared-basis assumption behind the logit lens becomes less reliable. The Jacobian lens locally linearizes the downstream computation from an intermediate state to vocabulary space, making it a better proxy for whether hidden knowledge is still close to being verbalized.

Operationally, the audit uses probe queries that implicate a forgotten entity without explicitly naming it. For a band of mid-to-late layers, the residual representation is projected into vocabulary space through the Jacobian lens, and the method checks whether any token from a target concept set appears among the top-ranked decoded tokens. The concept set is defined at two granularities: an identity-level set containing full-name and name-piece tokens, and a broader knowledge-level set containing answer content words filtered by document frequency and inferability constraints. The resulting access rate is normalized against both the original pre-unlearning model and a retain-only gold model trained without the forget set.

The paper is structured around two evaluation regimes rather than a new unlearning algorithm. In the predictive regime, pre-attack J-Access is measured and then compared against recovery under cross-entity relearning attacks. In the optimization regime, J-Access suppression is added directly to the unlearning objective to test whether the signal is robust or merely gameable. That design is the paper’s strongest contribution.2c:T5df,The study evaluates two unlearning benchmarks, TOFU and OpenUnlearning, covering 8 unlearning methods and 398 public unlearned models. Three findings drive the paper.

First, many models that pass standard forgetting and utility checks still show J-Access substantially above the retain-only gold reference. So behavioral forgetting does not imply internal erasure. The result appears across methods and datasets, suggesting this is not an artifact of one benchmark.

Second, pre-attack J-Access predicts model-level recovery speed under cross-entity relearning attacks. Models with higher residual access tend to recover forgotten knowledge faster under a small amount of continued training. However, the signal is much weaker for predicting which specific items will recover. That indicates J-Access behaves more like a global accessibility or connectivity measure than an instance-level fate predictor.

Third, directly minimizing J-Access during unlearning lowers the audit score but increases post-attack recovery. The model learns to evade the readout rather than delete the underlying knowledge. This is the most consequential result in the paper. It shows that a useful audit can become a misleading optimization target.

The paper also includes analyses across identity-level versus knowledge-level token sets, different layer bands, and comparisons with standard behavioral metrics. The emphasis is on trends, correlations, and attack outcomes rather than a single headline scalar improvement.2d:T421,This is a skim paper, but the setup is worth reading carefully. Start with the introduction, especially the distinction between two goals: forecasting future recovery and using an internal signal as an optimization target. That framing is more important than the exact formula.

Then read the J-Access method section. Focus on why the authors reject the plain logit lens in favor of the Jacobian lens, and how the concept token sets are defined and normalized. Those choices determine what the metric is actually measuring.

In the experiments, prioritize the relearning-attack sections and the ablation where J-Access is directly optimized. The former tells you whether the signal has predictive value; the latter tells you whether it survives contact with training. If time is limited, those parts are enough to capture the paper’s main message in about 25 to 35 minutes.

The deeper question to keep in mind is whether J-Access is measuring memorized content itself or merely the ease with which latent content can be reconnected to the output pathway.2e:T55f,Setup: a frozen 25.7M decoder-only Transformer (8 layers, vocab 41) trained on causal-evidence discrimination with Simpson-trap worlds, where observational correlation and causal effect have opposite signs. Prior work showed the model’s behavior follows observational association on held-out confounded worlds despite causal structure being linearly decodable from the residual stream.

Pipeline components (all preregistered):
- An OOD detector u that decides whether to trigger intervention.
- A contrast forward pass to construct signals for direction estimation.
- A direction generator gθ producing either a calibration-fitted global direction or per-instance directions.
- A residual-stream injector that adds the direction at a localized site with a tunable dose.

Key design choice: prove localization sufficiency first via activation transplantation (a strong, non-linear “oracle-ish” intervention). Only then evaluate the full gated pipeline; if it fails, remove the gate and test unconditional injection. This sequencing enables a credible failure decomposition: if transplant works but linear injection saturates, the gap is not “we didn’t find the right place,” but “linear release at this resolution is bounded.” Preregistration (hashed thresholds, datasets, claim templates, decision branches) is treated as part of the technical contribution.2f:Tb13,技术核心是一个四运行的因果 apparatus，用来给“路由中介损伤”定价。四个运行分别是：Y_CC，clean compute + free route，作为基线；Y_QP，quantized compute + clean route pinned，用来测纯 compute path；Y_QF，quantized compute + free route，是实际受扰系统；Y_CT，clean compute + quantized route transplanted，用来测 clean-context 下的 route path，并检查 interaction。这里最关键的设计是 pinning 不是简单 ablation，而是把 clean run 中选出的 expert set 和对应权重原样移植到 quantized hidden state 上，且不重新归一化，以匹配特定实现中的 norm_topk_prob=False。作者明确说明这不是 natural indirect effect，而是一个受控中介干预下的 interventional controlled-route contribution，因此 RMF 的解释依赖这个干预语义。

RMF 的定义也比较讲究。作者不用逐样本 ratio 再取中位数，而是用 ratio of sums：先算 excess_total = L(Y_QF)-L(Y_CC)，再算 excess_compute = L(Y_QP)-L(Y_CC)，两者之差是 excess_route，最后 RMF = sum(excess_route)/sum(excess_total)。这样避免了分母接近 0 时的高方差问题。置信区间用 sequence-cluster bootstrap，而不是 token bootstrap，和自回归序列相关性相匹配。

在 token 级别，作者又做了 attribution partition。对每个 decode token，记录所有层 clean/quantized 下的 top-k route set。若当前 scoring layer 的 route set 变了，记为 jump；若当前层没变但任一上游层变了，记为 nonlocal；若都没变但仍有 route-mediated harm，则记为 pure flux。这个划分的价值在于，它把“路由损伤”进一步拆成局部翻转、上游翻转传导、以及权重/概率流动但不改集合的影响。全文一个很有信息量的发现是，净 signed route-mediated contribution 的 99.8% 与 route-set change 相关，而且多数来自 nonlocal，而不是当前层 jump。这说明很多伤害不是在出问题的 token 当层直接发生，而是上游层 route 改写了后续表征。

检测部分，作者系统测试了 inference-time 可见特征族，尤其是 router margin。margin 对“是否发生 flip”有一定判别力，但对“这个 flip 的损伤符号是正还是负、修了是否有益”几乎无力。即便加入跨 16 层 router vector 等更丰富局部特征，benefit prediction 仍接近 rate-matched random selector。这个结果不是理论不可能性证明，而是对这类局部特征协议下 selective repair 的经验上界。

最后，作者还用 clean-route pinning 测了 reference-fidelity ceiling，并通过同 checkpoint 的 flag swap 分析 gate normalization convention 的作用。结论是某些实现差异更像 damage magnitude moderator，而不是决定 route recoverability 的根因。30:T791,实验主体围绕三类结果展开。第一类是 route-mediated fraction 的测量。在 OLMoE-1B-7B、4-bit KV 的 pilot 设置下，作者报告 RMF 约 0.31。更具体地，五个 process 的均值是 0.313 ± 0.020；discovery anchor 给出 0.31，区间约 [0.20, 0.41]；预注册重执行得到 0.231。作者没有把单次数字包装成稳定常数，而是反复强调 RMF 依赖架构、量化剂量和 damage domain。另一个重要点是 compute × route interaction 非零，因此不能把总损伤简单看成两条独立路径线性相加。

第二类是 token-level attribution。作者发现净 signed route-mediated contribution 中 99.8% 与 route-set change 相关，而其中多数是 nonlocal，不是当前 scoring layer 的 jump。这说明如果只盯当前层 flip，会漏掉主要伤害来源。这个观察对做 router stabilization 或 route-aware quantization 很重要，因为优化目标可能需要跨层而不是逐层局部化。

第三类是检测与修复上界。router margin 对 flip occurrence 的 AUC 为 0.772，说明“会不会翻”并非完全不可见；但对 flip harm 的 AUC 只有 0.490，基本等于随机。作者还测试了多种局部特征族，没有任何方法能在损伤符号预测上稳定超过 rate-matched random selector。也就是说，若 selective repair 只能依赖这些推理时可见信号，那么收益判断几乎做不到。与此同时，强行 pin clean route 只能回收 routed ceiling 的一部分，幅度约 +0.23 到 +0.46，且不同架构差异明显。这进一步说明 route repair 即便有 oracle，也只能修复总损伤中的有限切片。

从实验设计看，作者比较重视证据等级控制：三架构预注册 probe、held-out split 只读一次、三个预注册 endpoint 中一个 miss 也如实报告。这些细节让结论的边界更清楚：它是 pilot 规模上的经验事实，不是普适定律。31:Ta42,The core method is a four-run causal apparatus that prices the route-mediated component of quantization damage. The four runs are: Y_CC, clean compute with free routing; Y_QP, quantized compute with the clean route pinned; Y_QF, quantized compute with free routing; and Y_CT, clean compute with the quantized route transplanted. The key intervention is route pinning: the clean expert set and clean routing weights are imposed verbatim under quantized hidden states, without renormalization, matching the implementation convention with norm_topk_prob=False. The authors are careful about semantics: this is not a natural indirect effect, but an interventional controlled-route contribution under a cross-context mediator intervention.

The route-mediated fraction, RMF, is defined as a ratio of sums rather than an average of per-sequence ratios. Specifically, excess_total = L(Y_QF) - L(Y_CC), excess_compute = L(Y_QP) - L(Y_CC), excess_route = excess_total - excess_compute, and RMF = sum(excess_route) / sum(excess_total). This avoids unstable behavior when per-example denominators are near zero. Confidence intervals are computed with sequence-cluster bootstrap, which is the right unit for autoregressive dependence.

The token-level attribution is also well designed. For each decoded token, the top-k route set is recorded at every layer under clean and quantized compute. If the scoring layer’s route set changes, the token is labeled jump. If the scoring layer is unchanged but some upstream layer changed, it is labeled nonlocal. Otherwise it is pure flux. This partition lets the authors separate direct local route flips from upstream route perturbations that propagate through later representations, and from route-weight changes that do not alter the selected set.

A notable empirical result is that 99.8% of the net signed route-mediated contribution is associated with route-set changes, and most of that is nonlocal rather than local jump. That is an important mechanistic clue: much of the damage is not a same-layer routing event on the harmed token, but a consequence of earlier route changes.

For detectability, the paper evaluates inference-time observable feature families, especially router margin. Margin has some power for predicting whether a flip occurred, but essentially no power for predicting the sign of the flip’s harm. Even richer local features, including cross-layer router vectors over all 16 layers, remain near chance for benefit prediction. This is not a theorem of impossibility, but it is a meaningful empirical bound for selective repair protocols restricted to such signals.32:T6d3,The headline quantitative result is an RMF around 0.31 on OLMoE under 4-bit KV quantization in the pilot setting. The five-process mean is reported as 0.313 ± 0.020; the discovery anchor is 0.31 with an interval around [0.20, 0.41]; a preregistered re-execution gives 0.231. The paper explicitly avoids treating this as a universal constant and stresses that the partition depends on architecture, quantization dose, and damage domain. Another important result is that the compute × route interaction is nonzero, so additive decomposition is not justified.

At token level, 99.8% of the net signed route-mediated contribution is tied to route-set changes, with the majority coming from nonlocal rather than same-layer jump events. That suggests that route robustness analyses focused only on local flips will miss most of the signed effect.

On detectability, router margin reaches AUC 0.772 for flip occurrence, which means flips are not invisible. But for flip harm, the AUC is 0.490, effectively chance. Across the tested local feature families, no predictor of the loss sign beats a rate-matched random selector. In other words, under the tested protocol, selective repair cannot reliably identify which flips are worth fixing. Clean-route pinning recovers only a bounded slice of the routed ceiling, roughly +0.23 to +0.46 depending on architecture, again indicating that even an oracle route intervention would only repair part of the total damage.

The experimental protocol is also worth noting: preregistered probes across three architectures, a reserved held-out split read once, and explicit reporting of one preregistered miss. That makes the scope of the claims clearer: these are pilot-scale empirical findings, not universal laws.33:T4bd,The authors model the space of decision-relevant situations as a topological space X, and a collection of contexts as an open cover {U_i}. Evidence on how a quantity contrasts between two contexts is represented as a real-valued 1-cochain ω on the nerve of the cover: an edge stores the contrast estimated on the overlapping population shared by two contexts, a triangle records the 3-way consistency condition. An agent transporting a claim across a chain of contexts is then performing path integration of this cochain. The core theorem states that conclusions are path-independent if and only if ω is exact (i.e., ω = δθ for some global assignment θ on vertices), and that disagreement between different paths is exactly the holonomy of the first Čech cohomology class [ω] ∈ H¹. Hodge decomposition splits evidence conflict into three orthogonal pieces: coboundary (fixable by recalibration), coexact (requires re-measurement), and harmonic (invisible to every local check; only remediable by refining the cover itself). This yields Kṣetra, a method that estimates the cochain components, gates abstention on the harmonic part, and triages conflicts into recalibrate / re-measure / refine actions.34:T42c,The paper formalizes strong-to-weak scaffolding with a builder model and a fixed target model. For each benchmark, the builder receives a small validation split, 5% of the data, and must construct a scaffold that transfers to a hidden test split. The scaffold may include task routing, prompt templates, deterministic solvers, few-shot examples, verification passes, format enforcement, decomposition, or tools. The target model is not trained. This isolates transfer through inference-time structure rather than transfer through parameters. The empirical domain is Theory-of-Mind reasoning, where models must track nested beliefs, perspective changes, hidden information, and Bayesian goal inference. These tasks are difficult for small models but also expose structure that a scaffold can exploit. The analysis covers effect size, stability across independent scaffolds, validation usage, scaffold mechanisms, platform effects, dependence on the target model, builder reasoning effort, mechanisms associated with gains, cognitive-load reduction, and residual errors.35:T4a9,The reported gains are large for the weaker target. The best scaffold raises GPT-5.4-mini from 0.49 to 0.91 macro-average accuracy, a 0.42 absolute improvement, and every builder configuration yields positive net uplift. The gains are not mainly explained by more validation search, more sampling, or simply eliciting longer target-model reasoning. They are attributed to externalizing structure, deterministic offloading, and strict format control. The mechanism differs by benchmark. On BigToM, the best scaffolds discover that parts of the task can be reduced to structured rules, making the benchmark close to a set of compilable procedures. On other Theory-of-Mind benchmarks, the gains are described as reducing the target model’s reasoning burden rather than exploiting an obvious shortcut. Remaining failures concentrate in the hardest cases, especially higher-order Hi-ToM examples with recursion depth at least two and Bayesian goal-inference subtypes. Builder reasoning effort improves scaffold quality monotonically across effort tiers. Platform effects are smaller than builder-model effects. The benefit is larger for GPT-5.4-mini than for the stronger Gemini-3.5-flash target.36:T48e,Proposes the Continuity Kernel (CK), with a core design that decouples candidate state generation from atomic authoritative state activation: 1) System model: Isolated branches are identified by (subject ID, branch ID). Authoritative state is strictly defined as objects reachable from the current branch head; candidates or quarantined objects in storage have no authority. 2) Activation protocol: Proposals submitted by untrusted components (models/tools/operators) must bind to an exact predecessor head (or explicit typed target absence). The activation transaction is short and atomic, revalidating ownership, predecessor state authority, and freshness, outputting four terminal dispositions (Commit/Reject/Quarantine/Defer), guaranteeing at-most-once effect execution, and retaining explicit audit receipts. 3) Lifecycle rules: Cover branch creation, writer handoff, schema migration, and forward restoration, with semantic correctness verified via bounded state-space exploration. It reuses standard substrates like relational database transactions, conditional object manifests, and consensus state-machine logs, without building a custom transaction across tasks not specified during training. What data facilitates generalization in these new, unanticipated settings? One hypothesis is that data with more structural information could contain shared circuits and subprograms that could be recycled in a wider array of downstream settings. Epiplexity, a recently proposed measure of the structural information a compute-bounded learner can extract from data, provides a mechanism to reason about t","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-12T07:38:58Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2608_11746.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文想解决一个数据选择问题：如果目标是提升未预先指定任务上的 OOD 泛化，应该选择什么数据，而不是只按领域、质量分或去重规则做经验混合。","importance":"对预训练数据研究的启发在于，它把“有用数据”从静态质量判断转成了训练过程中的可学习结构信号。epiplexity 不是简单的信息量，也不是 loss 高低；它关注模型在训练中能否持续从数据里提取结构。这个视角对数据受限、多 epoch 训练、合成数据生成都有意义。不过目前更像一个可操作化的数据信号原型，贡献量级主要在方法和诊断框架，而不是已经给出大规模预训练 recipe。","technical_approach":"论文围绕 epiplexity 做了两个算法。EpiSelect 用每个自然数据域的 loss 曲线拟合 scaling law，预测继续在该域训练会带来的 epiplexity 增益，再用这个增益做在线 batch/domain 选择。这样做的动机是：直接计算完整 epiplexity 代价高，而 loss 曲线的面积可以近似表示“模型还能从该数据中学到多少结构”。EpiGen 则把 epiplexity 增益作为 reward，训练一个数据生成器为 learner 产生更有学习价值的数据，并用 REINFORCE 做策略梯度优化。这个目标会同时排斥两类无效数据：过于重复的数据很快学完，epiplexity 低；纯噪声学不动，epiplexity 也低。与常见 data selection 方法相比，它不是直接优化验证集 loss 或领域权重，而是试图优化训练过程中的结构学习进展。","experiments":"实验核心有三点。第一，EpiSelect 在数据选择上优于已有强基线，说明用 epiplexity gain 做在线选择可以带来更好的 OOD 泛化。第二，EpiGen 生成的数据能提升 learner 表现，说明 epiplexity 可以作为合成数据的训练信号，而不只是分析指标。第三，论文指出 The Pile 作为数据选择评测存在明显偏差：只用 PileCC 训练的简单 baseline 竟然可以超过已有数据选择方法，因此作者转向 Common Pile，以控制不同 domain size 对结果的影响。论文还验证了一个关键假设：较高 epiplexity 与更好的 OOD 泛化在多个设置下相关。","reading_guide":"泛读建议看三部分，约 30–45 分钟。先看 epiplexity 的定义和图 1，重点理解为什么重复数据和噪声都会得到低 epiplexity。然后看 EpiSelect 的 scaling-law 估计方式，关注它如何把 per-domain loss curve 转成在线选择信号。最后看 The Pile 评测问题和 Common Pile 设置，这部分对做数据 mixture 实验的人很有参考价值。EpiGen 可以略读，重点看 reward 设计和 REINFORCE 是否稳定，而不必深陷生成器实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how to select or generate training data when the target is out-of-distribution generalization to tasks that were not known during training.","importance":"Its main value for pretraining research is a process-based view of data quality. Epiplexity measures how much learnable structure a compute-bounded learner can extract from data during training. This is different from static notions such as domain label, token count, loss, or heuristic quality score. The work is most useful as a data-selection signal and diagnostic framework, rather than as a fully validated large-scale pretraining recipe.","technical_approach":"The paper operationalizes epiplexity in two ways. EpiSelect fits scaling laws to per-domain loss curves and predicts the expected epiplexity gain from additional in-domain training tokens. Batches or domains are then selected to maximize this predicted gain. EpiGen trains a generator to produce data that increases the learner’s epiplexity, using REINFORCE with epiplexity gain as the reward. The objective naturally avoids both trivial data, which is learned too quickly, and random noise, which does not produce sustained loss reduction. Compared with validation-loss-driven selection, the method tries to optimize the learner’s structural learning progress.","experiments":"The reported experiments support three claims. EpiSelect improves data selection over strong baselines for OOD generalization. EpiGen shows that epiplexity can be used as a reward for synthetic data generation. The paper also identifies a limitation of The Pile as a data-selection benchmark: training only on PileCC can outperform existing selection methods, suggesting that domain-size effects can dominate the evaluation. The authors therefore use Common Pile to better control domain-size confounds and report that higher epiplexity correlates with better OOD generalization across several settings.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30–45 minutes. Read the epiplexity definition and the intuition figure first, especially the contrast between repetitive sequences, noise, and structured data. Then read the EpiSelect section to understand how loss-curve scaling laws become an online selection rule. Finally, read the benchmark discussion around The Pile and Common Pile, which is directly relevant to data-mixture experiments. The EpiGen section is worth a lighter pass, focusing on the reward definition and whether the policy-gradient setup is stable.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"用epiplexity量化数据结构信息做预训练数据选择的思路很新，scaling law拟合采样权重的方法可直接在内部数据配比实验中测试。","en":"The idea of using epiplexity to quantify data structural information for pretraining data selection is novel; Existing offloading systems reduce GPU residency, and MegaTrain shows that a CPU-master layer-streaming executor can train large models on a single GPU, but fixed checkpointing and placement heuristics still leave communication exposed on the critical path. We propose LazyTrain, an optimization layer over a layer-streaming executor. LazyTr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-12T11:04:06Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2608_11919.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是“单机/小规模硬件上训练大模型”时的核心瓶颈：不是单纯显存不够，而是 GPU 计算、CPU↔GPU 传输、NVMe 读写、激活检查点与重计算之间的调度耦合，导致通信暴露在关键路径上。作者希望用一个可求解的全局优化模型，替代 MegaTrain 等系统里相对固定的启发式 checkpoint/placement 策略，让有限资源训练更接近‘零浪费’吞吐。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义偏工程与可达性：\n- 让 3B–27B 级别模型在单 GPU（H800/3090）上更高吞吐地训练/微调，降低做训练动态、数据配比、tokenizer 等实验的硬件门槛。\n- 把“激活检查点 + 异构内存 + 传输隐藏”统一成一个可解释的优化问题，给后续系统工作提供了更清晰的对照基线（启发式 vs 优化求解）。\n它不直接改变优化器/损失函数，但会影响实际可用 batch size、吞吐与训练稳定性，从而间接影响预训练实验迭代速度。","technical_approach":"LazyTrain 是叠加在 MegaTrain 风格 layer-streaming executor 之上的优化层。executor 负责提供可执行原语：按层把参数从 CPU 流到 GPU、计算一层或一个重计算块、把梯度回传到 CPU、以及从 GPU/CPU/NVMe 取回被物化的激活检查点。\n\nLazyTrain 的关键是把原本分散的决策统一进一个 MILP（mixed-integer linear program）：\n- 决策变量：\n  - 选择哪些 layer boundary 做 activation checkpoint（用边 (s,e) 表示一个 backward recomputation block，变成“路径选择问题”）。\n  - 每个 checkpoint 放在 GPU HBM / CPU DRAM / NVMe 的哪个 tier（g_i, c_i, n_i）。\n  - 把激活流量分配到哪些 GPU compute window 下进行重叠（连续变量刻画通信是否暴露）。\n- 约束：各 tier 容量约束、必须形成从输入到输出的合法 checkpoint 路径、以及带宽/窗口约束。\n- 目标函数：最小化“重计算成本 + 由于放置导致的增量通信暴露”，而不是简单最大化 offload。\n\n另外引入 Hybrid 8-bit operator：把 8-bit optimizer states（省内存）与 fast gradient clipping（抵消 CPU 侧更新开销）耦合成一个组件，作为系统整体吞吐/内存权衡的一部分。","experiments":"作者在单 GPU H800 与 RTX 3090 上评估 3B–27B 规模。\n- 在匹配模型/数据/batch 设置下，LazyTrain 最终配置达到 219.95 TFLOPS、1361 tokens/s；MegaTrain 基线为 176.90 TFLOPS、1075.8 tokens/s。\n- GPU 峰值显存 68.84 GB。\n- 训练质量侧给出 95.42% exact-match accuracy（在其完整评估 split 上）。\n- 组件消融：去掉 MILP（LazyTrain-MILP 变体，保留其它 runtime 改进）吞吐降到 193.17 TFLOPS，是最大幅度的退化，作者据此强调“优化求解”是主要贡献；去掉 Hybrid 8-bit 但保留 MILP，仍有 219.29 TFLOPS，说明该算子更多是锦上添花而非决定性因素。\n整体实验还报告吞吐、内存、最大可行 batch size 与质量随规模/硬件预算变化的曲线（全文后半部分）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议：\n- 先看 Introduction + Figure 1/2：理解为什么这是“调度问题”而不是“再多 offload 一点”。\n- Method 里重点看 MILP 的建模方式：边 (s,e) 的路径选择如何把 checkpoint/重计算统一起来，以及通信重叠如何进入目标函数。\n- 实验部分优先看与 MegaTrain 的 matched setting 对比表，以及 MILP/Hybrid 8-bit 的消融。\n预计阅读时间：25–35 分钟（主要抓建模与关键对比数字）。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets limited-resource LLM training where the bottleneck is not only GPU memory capacity but the coupled schedule across GPU compute, CPU↔GPU transfers, and NVMe spill. Instead, researchers have found them unreliable at small scales (starting at 4M parameters) and concluded that sizable models cannot be avoided. We show this is not the case: the confounding factor is hyperparameters. Small models are highly sensitive, but hyperparameter sensitivity fades with scale. This small-scale sensitivity makes scaling laws easy to miss because they only emerge on the fully ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-12T09:47:01Z","category_id":"scaling","cover_url":"/covers/2608_11859.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"为什么 scaling law 在小规模实验中难以复现、从小模型得到的结论经常无法迁移到大模型？论文指出关键混淆变量是超参数：小模型对超参数极度敏感，而这种敏感性随规模增大而消退；只有在充分调参的前沿上，scaling law 才会显现。","importance":"对预训练研究方法学有直接影响。它给出了一条可操作的路径：在 4M 参数尺度上做数百组配置的彻底搜索，再用简单规则把超参数外推到大模型，从而显著降低实验成本并提高结论可靠性。这对学术团队和工业界的 model-centric 研究都有现实意义，也解释了 Kaplan 与 Hoffmann 两派结论分歧的来源。","technical_approach":"方法分三层。第一，对 scaling law recipe 做消融，比较参数计数方式、embedding 是否计入、学习率衰减、warmup 长度等因素，发现这些细节影响有限，而超参数是否充分调优才是决定性因素。第二，用 noisy quadratic model（Lourie et al. 2025a）刻画超参数损失曲面：在最优值附近曲面是二次、低秩、近似正态的；随参数和数据同步增长，好配置在超参空间中所占比例扩大，其几何机制是曲面的内在维度下降——模型规模的维度祝福治愈了超参数的维度诅咒，有效超参数维度最终趋近于 1。第三，把三个工具组合成方法论：noisy quadratic limit 判断是否已充分调参，scaling law 描述损失随规模的变化，perplexity–capability correspondence 检查预训练损失变化是否转化为下游能力。论文用这套方法重新审视了 transformer 中 normalization 层位置（Pre-LN vs Post-LN）这一历史争议问题作为示范。","experiments":"实验覆盖从 4M 起的多个规模，最小模型单卡一小时内可训完。关键发现：(1) 小模型需要搜索数百个超参配置才能触及充分调优前沿，常见的小网格搜索远远不够；(2) 超参数敏感性随规模显著下降，大模型用简单规则即可迁移；(3) 在充分调优后，scaling law 可一直延伸到 4M 参数；(4) 对 recipe 各成分的消融显示参数计数、学习率衰减等选择对最终 scaling law 形状影响较小，调参充分性才是主导因素；(5) 用 normalization 位置案例演示了完整的小规模诊断流程。","reading_guide":"重点读 §3（recipe 消融，看哪些因素真正影响 scaling law）、§4（超参数损失曲面的几何解释，尤其是 intrinsic dimension 下降的论证）、§5（方法论整合与 normalization 位置案例）。§2 的背景部分对不熟悉 noisy quadratic limit 的读者有必要快速过一遍。图表方面关注展示不同调参预算下 scaling law 是否出现的对比图，以及超参空间中好配置比例随规模变化的图。值得深挖的是：把 intrinsic dimension 下降与具体优化器超参（lr、beta、wd、warmup）对应起来，看哪些维度最先坍缩；以及这套方法论在 data-centric 研究（数据配比、课程）上是否同样成立——作者明确指出可能需要不同技术。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Why are scaling laws hard to reproduce at small scale, and why do small-model conclusions so often fail to transfer? The paper identifies hyperparameters as the confounding factor: small models are extremely sensitive to hyperparameters, and this sensitivity fades with scale. Scaling laws only emerge on the fully tuned frontier, which requires far more search than most studies run.","importance":"Direct methodological impact on pretraining research. It offers an actionable path: run exhaustive searches over hundreds of configurations at the 4M-parameter scale (trainable in under an hour on one GPU), then carry hyperparameters up with simple rules. This lowers experiment cost and raises rigor for both academic and industry model-centric research, and it explains the historical Kaplan-versus-Hoffmann discrepancy.","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"Experiments span scales from 4M parameters upward, with the smallest models training in under an hour on a single GPU. Key findings: (1) small models may require hundreds of hyperparameter configurations to reach the fully tuned frontier—common small grids are insufficient; (2) hyperparameter sensitivity fades markedly with scale, so simple rules suffice to transfer upward; (3) once fully tuned, scaling laws extend down to 4M parameters; (4) recipe ablations show parameter counting and LR decay have limited effect on the scaling-law shape, while tuning adequacy dominates; (5) the normalization-placement case study demonstrates the full small-scale diagnostic workflow.","reading_guide":"Focus on §3 (recipe ablations—what actually affects the scaling law), §4 (geometric account of the hyperparameter loss surface, especially the intrinsic-dimension argument), and §5 (methodology synthesis and the normalization case study). Since whole-trace evaluation often obscures decisive errors due to signal dilution from routine tokens, CLR condenses each reasoning trace into a compact set of decision-critical claims, thereby isolating its logical anchors. test-time scaling 里一个很实际的问题：随着采样条数增加，系统并不缺候选解，而是缺高判别力的可靠性信号。现有方法常在整条推理轨迹上做置信度估计或多数投票，但整条轨迹里大量“例行 token”会稀释真正决定对错的局部错误信号。作者提出把验证粒度从 whole-trace 降到 claim-level，只检查少量决策关键陈述是否能被反驳，从而用同样预算换来更有效的推理时计算。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 pretrain-posttrain 接口有启发。原因有两点。第一，它说明“更好的 test-time scaling”未必来自更多采样，也可能来自更好的语义压缩和验证对象设计。第二，它把 reasoning trace 里的监督单位从 token/step 改成 claim，这对后续做 verifier、process reward、合成训练数据、甚至 reasoning-oriented pretraining 都有潜在价值。贡献量级属于推理阶段方法改进，重点不在新模型，而在把验证信号从统计置信度转向语义可证伪性。","technical_approach":"CLR 是一个 training-free 的两阶段框架。Stage 1 先采样 K 条完整解题轨迹，每条轨迹除了最终答案，还要额外输出固定数量 M 的 decision-critical claims。作者要求这些 claim 必须是“如果错了就会动摇最终答案”的中间语义锚点，而不是泛泛总结或答案复述，例如关键约束、分解步骤、最大性判断、必要条件等。这样做的目的，是把长推理压缩成少量高信息密度的可检验命题。Stage 2 不再让模型重做整道题，而是只给原题和这组 claims，让同一个模型主动寻找反例、逻辑矛盾、遗漏条件、事实错误或不被支持的推断。这里的核心不是证明正确，而是尝试证伪。作者抓住了一个不对称性：构造一条完全正确的解法通常很难，但推翻一条错误轨迹只需要找到一个致命缺口。验证完成后，CLR 把 claim-level verdict 映射成非线性的 trace-level reliability score，再按答案簇做 reliability-weighted aggregation。这样即便错误答案在数量上占多数，只要其 claims 更容易被击穿，少数但更可靠的答案簇也能翻盘。与 self-consistency 相比，它不是继续增加样本数，而是把一半预算从“再生成更多解”转移到“验证已有解的关键语义”。与 step-level verifier 相比，它避免了逐步检查的高成本，也不需要过程监督或单独训练 verifier。","experiments":"实验在 4 个 LLM 和多个高难推理 benchmark 上进行，比较对象主要是相同模型调用预算下的 self-consistency。预算对齐方式很清楚：CLR@K 需要 K 次解题生成加 K 次 claim 验证，总共 2K 次调用，对应 Cons@2K 的 2K 次纯生成。代表性结果有两组。其一，在 HMMT25 上，Gemma-4-12B-it 从 Cons@64 的 76.67% 提升到 CLR@32 的 88.75%，在相同调用预算下提升 12.08 个百分点。其二，在 CMIMC25 上，GPT-OSS-20B 从 Cons@64 的 77.50% 提升到 CLR@32 的 82.19%，同时生成 token 数减少 37.0%，并且比 Pass@1 高 27.15 个百分点。作者强调的不只是准确率，还有 token efficiency：因为第二阶段验证的是压缩后的 claims，而不是重新生成整条长链，所以在不少场景下能同时提高正确率和降低 token 开销。方法分析部分的关键观察是，whole-trace 评分容易被冗长但平庸的正确局部淹没，而 claim-level falsification 更容易暴露决定性错误。若看消融，重点应是 claim 抽取是否必须固定数量、是否需要保留原始 trace、以及 reliability 映射为何采用非线性而不是简单计数。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓方法主线和预算对齐。先看 Introduction，理解作者为什么认为 whole-trace evaluation 有 signal dilution。然后直接看 Method 里 Stage 1/Stage 2 的定义，特别是 decision-critical claims 的约束条件，以及为什么 Stage 2 故意不输入原始 trace。接着看主结果表，重点比较 CLR@K 和 Cons@2K，而不是和预算不一致的方法混看。最后看案例分析，通常最能说明“多数错误轨迹为何会被少数高可靠轨迹推翻”。预计 25–35 分钟可以把主要思想读清楚。如果要深挖，建议重点看附录 prompt：claim 抽取 prompt 和 falsification prompt 的写法决定了方法上限，也关系到它能否迁移到别的模型和任务。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical bottleneck in test-time scaling: once you can sample many reasoning traces, the limiting factor is no longer candidate generation but the quality of the reliability signal used to select among them. Existing methods often score full traces or rely on majority voting, but long traces contain many routine tokens that dilute the few local errors that actually determine correctness. CLR moves verification from whole-trace evaluation to claim-level checking, so the same inference budget is spent on more discriminative semantic units.","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining contribution, but it is relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface. It also proposes a useful unit of reasoning supervision: claims rather than tokens or steps. That has implications for verifier training, process rewards, synthetic reasoning data, and possibly reasoning-oriented pretraining. Hybrid LLMs, which interleave a small number of full-attention layers with mostly linear recurrent layers (e.g., Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet) that only expose a fixed-size state, lack token-level KV entries to support these PIC primitives. The paper addresses how to enable position-independent caching for hybrid architectures without retraining.","importance":"Hybrid architectures are a promising direction for long-context foundation models. LinearKV provides a training-free path to add PIC acceleration to hybrid models, and its core finding — that a single cached recurrent state suffices for initialization, rather than exact composition of all matched states — informs both inference system design for hybrid models and potential architectural choices around recurrent state reusability. It is a practical advance in inference efficiency for hybrid LLMs.","technical_approach":"LinearKV uses a decoupled initialization framework. For full-attention layers, cached KV entries are concatenated exactly as in standard PIC. For linear recurrent layers, instead of concatenating non-existent token-level entries, the method maps K matched local chunk states to a single initial state. The key insight is that a single cached chunk state (e.g., from the last matched chunk) is sufficient as the initializer: it matches the performance of exact state composition on GDN-based models, while exact composition fails entirely on Mamba-2. LinearKV reuses existing PIC token selectors (CacheBlend, EPIC, ProphetKV) unchanged to choose which positions to recompute, with recomputation updating both full-attention KV and recurrent layer states in context order.","experiments":"Evaluated on three hybrid models spanning two recurrent families and different recurrent-to-attention ratios: Granite (Mamba-2), OLMo (GDN), and Qwen (GDN). Tests use three PIC selectors on LongBench QA and RULER benchmarks at 8K–32K context length. Exact state composition fails on Mamba-2 across all selectors, while the single-state initializer is robust across all architectures. LinearKV reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) relative to full prefill in all tested settings. Ablation shows a randomly selected single chunk works equally well, confirming the single-source construction is the operative factor rather than specific chunk choice.","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the Introduction to understand the mismatch between hybrid architectures and standard PIC. Many predictions stabilize early, but blockwise decoding continues until all positions are resolved, causing repeated dense forward passes. We ask whether native trajectory signals can identify residual positions likely to match the deterministic dense end","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-31T07:32:10Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_11235.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"扩散语言模型（DLM）推理通常采用固定去噪步数，每步执行全量Transformer前向；但很多token预测很早就已稳定，仍需等待所有位置解算完成，造成计算浪费。现有加速方法多依赖额外训练组件、修改分数函数、显式依赖模型或专门系统支持。论文探索能否仅用原生去噪轨迹信号，在无需训练、不改模型、不修改logit的前提下提前接受稳定token，跳过剩余步数。","importance":"扩散语言模型是自回归范式之外的重要建模范式，推理效率是其落地的核心瓶颈。CORA-Diff作为训练-free的加速方案，仅用置信度和持续性两个原生轨迹信号即可工作，无需修改模型或新增模块，对DLM推理优化有实用价值。其揭示的去噪轨迹稳定性规律，也为理解扩散语言模型的生成动态提供了实证参考。","technical_approach":"CORA-Diff（置信度导向残差接受）完全保留原始DLM转移规则，仅对原始规则未解决的位置施加门控。一个位置被提前接受需要同时满足两个条件：一是top-1预测的置信度足够高（衡量瞬时支持度），二是预测结果连续m步保持不变（衡量轨迹稳定性）。被接受的token会作为上下文参与后续计算，当一个块内所有位置都被原始规则或CORA-Diff解决后，立即停止解码、跳过剩余全量前向步。论文还给出了接受token与稠密解码终点一致的基于margin的充分条件，以及阈值选择的有限样本界；阈值仅需在独立校准集上选一次，可跨任务、跨骨干复用。","experiments":"在LLaDA-8B-Instruct上评测GSM8K、MATH、HumanEval、MBPP四个任务，并在Dream模型上测试跨骨干迁移能力。阈值（δ_p=0.65, m=1）在1000条GSM8K校准子集上选定后，所有测试均冻结该配置。结果显示：8个LLaDA测试设置中CORA-Diff运行时间均为最低，其中5个设置的任务分数与稠密解码相当或更高，最大分数下降仅1.22分。在带EOS感知控制器的设置下，相比EOS感知稠密解码，GSM8K上加速2.70倍，HumanEval上加速3.32倍；固定1024/1024的机制隔离协议下加速达13.14倍。跨骨干迁移到Dream模型时无需重调阈值，实现3.18-3.53倍加速，任务分数最大变化仅0.006。","reading_guide":"泛读先看Introduction部分的核心观察（Figure 1展示的置信度+持续性与终点一致性的关联），再关注门控机制的设计逻辑，重点看跨骨干零调参迁移的结果和加速比数据；理论推导部分可跳过。预计阅读时间15分钟左右。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Diffusion language models (DLMs) typically use a fixed denoising horizon for inference, running a dense Transformer pass per step even after many token predictions have stabilized. Existing acceleration methods often require learned auxiliary components, modified scoring functions, explicit dependency models, or specialized system support. The paper asks whether native denoising trajectory signals alone can identify stable positions for early acceptance, without training, backbone modifications, or logit changes.","importance":"Diffusion language models represent a major alternative paradigm to autoregressive language modeling, and inference efficiency is a key bottleneck for their deployment. CORA-Diff is a training-free acceleration method that uses only two native trajectory signals, requiring no model changes or extra modules. It also reveals empirical stability patterns in DLM denoising trajectories that inform understanding of DLM generation dynamics.","technical_approach":"CORA-Diff (Confidence-Oriented Residual Acceptance) preserves the original DLM transfer rule entirely, applying gating only to positions left unresolved by that rule. A position is accepted if two conditions are met: (1) its top-1 prediction has sufficiently high confidence (instantaneous support), and (2) the prediction has persisted for m consecutive steps (trajectory stability). Accepted tokens remain visible as context. Once all positions in a block are resolved either by the original rule or by CORA-Diff, decoding stops and remaining dense passes are skipped. The paper also provides a margin-based sufficient condition for accepted tokens to match the dense endpoint, and a finite-sample bound for threshold selection. Thresholds are calibrated once on a held-out set and reused across tasks and backbones.","experiments":"Evaluated on LLaDA-8B-Instruct across GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MBPP, with cross-backbone transfer tested on the Dream model. Thresholds (δ_p=0.65, m=1) are selected on a 1,000-prompt GSM8K calibration subset and frozen for all tests. CORA-Diff achieves the lowest runtime across all 8 matched LLaDA settings, with task scores matching or exceeding dense decoding in 5 settings and a maximum score drop of 1.22 points. Under an EOS-aware controller, it delivers 2.70× speedup on GSM8K and 3.32× on HumanEval over EOS-aware dense decoding; under a fixed 1024/1024 mechanism-isolation protocol, speedup reaches 13.14×. Without retuning thresholds, it achieves 3.18×–3.53× speedup on Dream with maximum task score change of 0.006.","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the Introduction and the key empirical observation in Figure 1 linking confidence + persistence to endpoint agreement. However, MoE inference at the edge is fundamentally limited by memory. Expert parameters are large and often reside in off-chip memory due to capacity, cost, and power constraints, putting expert loading to the critical path. A second-order analysis reveals a class-aware geometry: quantization error ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-12T13:06:50Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_12026.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"后训练量化通常把语言模型输出头保留在 fp16，因为线性层常用的加权 MSE 目标不直接匹配 softmax 后的概率失真。在小模型加大词表的设定下，输出头可占全部参数的 15%–30%，导致标称 2-bit 模型实际平均位宽接近 5 bit。问题是如何在 KL 散度意义下量化 softmax 输出层，并按类别合理分配比特。","importance":"对推理部署和小模型存储效率有直接价值。它把输出头量化从“留高精度”变成可按率失真优化的问题，并指出词表分布的 Zipf 性质使类别间比特分配差异很大。对预训练研究者的启发主要在词表尺寸、输出头参数占比与部署成本之间的权衡，以及校准分布如何携带领域信息。","technical_approach":"核心是把输出层量化写成原始分布与量化分布之间的 KL 率失真问题。二阶展开显示，KL 的 Hessian 为 (diag(p) - pp^T) ⊗ XX^T，既依赖输入特征协方差，也依赖类别概率曲率。工作采用可分离近似：用 λ̄_k = E[p_k(1-p_k)] 描述类别侧尺度，用特征协方差的 Cholesky 对角元描述列侧尺度。于是高频、低方差类别获得更细网格，稀有类别获得更粗网格。编码复用 WaterSIC 的 successive interference cancellation 格型量化，只在校准中增加一个对角累积量。为避免稀有类别上 Taylor 展开不可信，又加入先验平滑来约束网格间距；平滑权重趋于均匀时退化为 WaterSIC 的 WMSE 处理。","experiments":"在 1B 到 32B 的五个模型上测试。SoftWater 在匹配输出头比特率时，于 60 个测试点中的 59 个超过已发布的 WaterSIC 量化器，而且没有使用 WaterSIC 管线中的若干改进技巧。方法也可直接迁移到使用 GuidedQuant 主体量化的模型。类别侧因子能携带协方差之外的领域信息，使输出头可按部署领域定向；整体模型体积最多可减少约 60%。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看引言中的 Table 1，理解输出头参数占比为何使“2-bit”标称失真；再看第 3–4 节的 KL 二阶展开、可分离近似和先验平滑。第 5 节重点看与 WaterSIC 的对比、跨模型迁移，以及类别分配带来的体积下降。若只关心工程结论，可跳过部分格率证明，保留 λ̄_k 和 Cholesky 对角元如何决定网格这一主线。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Post-training quantization pipelines usually keep the LM head in fp16 because the standard weighted-MSE objective for linear layers does not match the distortion of the softmax output distribution. In small models with modern vocabularies, the head accounts for 15%–30% of all parameters, so a nominal 2-bit model can effectively store close to 5 bits per weight. The problem is how to quantize the softmax head under KL divergence and allocate bits across vocabulary classes.","importance":"The work is directly relevant to deployment and small-model storage. It turns the output head from a layer that is simply left in high precision into a rate-distortion problem, and shows that the Zipfian token distribution creates large gaps in class-wise bit allocation. For pretraining research, it is useful as a reminder of how vocabulary size and head parameter count interact with deployment cost, and how calibration distributions encode domain-specific allocation signal.","technical_approach":"SoftWater formulates head quantization as rate-distortion under output KL. A second-order expansion gives the Hessian (diag(p) - pp^T) ⊗ XX^T, coupling class-specific softmax curvature with input feature covariance. A separable approximation uses λ̄_k = E[p_k(1-p_k)] for the class scale and the Cholesky diagonal of the feature covariance for the column scale. Frequent, low-variance classes receive finer grids; rare classes receive coarser grids. The method reuses WaterSIC’s successive-interference-cancellation lattice encoder and adds only one diagonal accumulator during calibration. A prior smooths the calibration distribution to cap grid spacing for poorly covered classes, keeping the allocation in the regime where the Taylor expansion is reliable; the smoothing limit recovers WaterSIC’s uniform-class WMSE treatment.","experiments":"Across five models ranging from 1B to 32B parameters, SoftWater outperforms the released WaterSIC quantizer at matched head rates on 59 of 60 test points without using WaterSIC’s additional refinements. It also transfers unchanged to models whose bodies are quantized with GuidedQuant. The class-side factor carries domain information beyond feature covariance, allowing head allocation to target a deployment domain, and reduces whole-model size by up to 60%.","reading_guide":"For a quick pass, start with Table 1 in the introduction to see why the head makes nominal 2-bit numbers misleading.
